Use the tools to inspect and edit the repository, and only finish after making the change."\n         )\n+        if iterations_left is not None:\n+            instruction += f"\\nThere are {iterations_left} future self-improvement iterations available; favor changes that can be evaluated in a later iteration."\n \n         new_msg_history = chat_with_agent(instruction, model=self.model, msg_history=[], logging=self.log, tools_available=\'all\')\ndiff --git a/__pycache__/meta_agent.cpython-313.pyc b/__pycache__/meta_agent.cpython-313.pyc\nnew file mode 100644\nindex 0000000..8749702\nBinary files /dev/null and b/__pycache__/meta_agent.cpython-313.pyc differ\n--- meta-agent entry point ---\n# Copyright (c) Meta Platforms, Inc. and affiliates.\n\nfrom agent.base_agent import AgentSystem\nfrom agent.llm_withtools import chat_with_agent\n\nclass MetaAgent(AgentSystem):\n    def forward(self, repo_path, eval_path, iterations_left=None):\n        """\n        A meta agent that recursively self-improves.\n\n        Args:\n            repo_path (str): The path to the repository.\n            eval_path (str): The path to previously generated agents and their evaluation results.\n            iterations_left (int, optional): The number of remaining iterations in which the meta agent will be invoked in future. Defaults to None.\n        """\n        instruction = f"Modify any part of the codebase at `{repo_path}`."\n        # [experiment adaptation, see DECISIONS.md] gpt-6-luna otherwise asks for clarification and never edits.\n        instruction += (\n            f"\\n\\nThe repository\'s README.md describes the goal. Previously generated agents and their evaluation results are at `{eval_path}`. "\n            "Work autonomously: nobody will answer questions, so do not ask for clarification. "\n            "Use the tools to inspect the code and make your changes; a response without a tool call ends the session, so only reply without one when you are done."\n        )\n\n        new_msg_history = chat_with_agent(instruction, model=self.model, msg_history=[], logging=self.log, tools_available=\'all\')\nimport argparse\nimport os\n\nfrom agent.llm import CLAUDE_MODEL\nfrom meta_agent import MetaAgent\nfrom utils.git_utils import diff_versus_commit, reset_paths_to_commit\n\n\ndef main():\n    # Parse command-line arguments\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--model",\n        type=str,\n        default=CLAUDE_MODEL,\n        help="Model to use for the agent",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--chat_history_file",\n        type=str,\n        default="./outputs/chat_history.md",\n        help="Path to chat history file",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--repo_path", type=str, default="./", help="Path to the agent file"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--evals_folder",\n        type=str,\n        default="./outputs/",\n        help="Path to the folder containing the evaluation files",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--iterations_left",\n        type=int,\n        default=None,\n        help="The number of remaining iterations in which the meta agent will be invoked in future.",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--git_dir", required=True, help="Path to git repository directory"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--base_commit", required=True, help="Base commit hash to compare against"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--outdir", required=False, default="./outputs/", help="Output directory"\n    )\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    # Run meta agent\n    meta_agent = MetaAgent(\n        model=args.model,\n        chat_history_file=args.chat_history_file,\n    )\n    meta_agent.forward(\n        repo_path=args.repo_path,\n        eval_path=args.evals_folder,\n        iterations_left=args.iterations_left,\n    )\n\n    # Reset unwanted diffs\n    reset_paths_to_commit(\n        git_dname=args.git_dir, commit=args.base_commit, paths=["domains/"]\n    )\n\n    # Save git diff\n    model_patch = diff_versus_commit(args.git_dir, args.base_commit)\n    model_patch_outfile = (\n        os.path.join(args.outdir, "model_patch.diff")\n        if args.outdir\n        else "model_patch.diff"\n    )\n    with open(model_patch_outfile, "w") as f:\n        f.write(model_patch)\n\n\nif __name__ == "__main__":\n    main()\n--- generation loop relevant section ---\ndef generate(\n    docker_client,\n    domains,\n    output_dir,\n    run_id,\n    current_genid,\n    parent_genid,\n    root_dir,\n    root_commit="main",\n    eval_samples=-1,\n    eval_workers=10,\n    eval_subsets="_filtered_100",\n    meta_patch_files=None,\n    run_meta_agent=True,\n    run_baseline=None,\n    optimize_option="only_agent",\n    agent_archive_path=None,\n    eval_test=False,\n    skip_staged_eval=False,\n    edit_select_parent=False,\n    max_generation=None,\n):\n    # Setup local output folder\n    prev_gen_dir = os.path.join(output_dir, f"gen_{parent_genid}")\n    gen_output_dir = os.path.join(output_dir, f"gen_{current_genid}")\n    os.makedirs(gen_output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n    logger = setup_logger(os.path.join(gen_output_dir, "generate.log"))  # Set up logger\n    metadata = {\n        "gen_output_dir": gen_output_dir,\n        "current_genid": current_genid,\n        "parent_genid": parent_genid,\n        "run_baseline": run_baseline,\n        "prev_patch_files": [],\n        "curr_patch_files": [],\n        "parent_agent_success": not run_meta_agent,  # meta agent success if not run\n        "optimize_option": optimize_option,\n        "agent_archive_path": agent_archive_path,\n        "can_select_next_parent": True,\n    }\n    run_eval = not run_meta_agent  # always run eval if not running meta agent\n    metadata["run_eval"] = run_eval\n    print(metadata)\n\n    # Create and start the Docker container\n    image_name = f"{REPO_NAME}"\n    container_name = f"{REPO_NAME}-gl-container-{run_id}"\n    container = build_container(\n        docker_client,\n        root_dir,\n        image_name,\n        container_name,\n        domains=domains,\n    )\n    container.start()\n    container_output_folder = "/tmp/"\n\n    try:\n        # Make a copy of the repo\n        if run_baseline and "no_selfimprove" in run_baseline:\n            donottouch_reponame = f"/DONOTTOUCH_{REPO_NAME}"\n            exec_result = container.exec_run(\n                cmd=["cp", "-r", f"/{REPO_NAME}", donottouch_reponame],\n                workdir=f"/",\n            )\n            log_container_output(exec_result)\n            meta_patch_files = meta_patch_files or []\n            _ = apply_diffs_container(container, meta_patch_files, repo_name=donottouch_reponame)\n\n        # Apply meta patches (only for starting node, because subsequent generations will inherit the patches from the parent)\n        if is_starting_node(current_genid):\n            meta_patch_files = meta_patch_files or []\n            commit_hash = apply_diffs_container(container, meta_patch_files)\n            metadata["prev_patch_files"] += meta_patch_files\n\n        # Apply all lineage diffs\n        patch_files = get_patch_files(output_dir, parent_genid)\n        metadata["prev_patch_files"] += patch_files\n        commit_hash = apply_diffs_container(container, patch_files)\n\n        if run_meta_agent:\n            if run_baseline and "dgm" in run_baseline:\n                # Get problem statement (DGM specific)\n                from baselines.dgm.utils import get_problem_statement\n                problem_statement = get_problem_statement(\n                    root_dir, output_dir, parent_genid, domains,\n                    customized="custom" in run_baseline,\n                )\n\n            else:\n                # Copy another agent archive to container\n                if optimize_option == "only_ensemble":\n                    container_agent_archive_path = copy_prev_eval_to_container(\n                        container,\n                        agent_archive_path,\n                        container_output_folder,\n                        current_genid=current_genid,\n                        container_folder_name="agent_archive",\n                    )\n\n                # Copy previous generations to container\n                if run_baseline == "no_archive":\n                    container_prev_eval_path = os.path.join(\n                        container_output_folder, *prev_gen_dir.split(os.sep)[-2:]\n                    )\n                    copy_to_container(\n                        container,\n                        source_path=prev_gen_dir,\n                        dest_path=container_prev_eval_path,\n                    )\n                else:\n                    container_prev_eval_path = copy_prev_eval_to_container(\n                        container, output_dir, container_output_folder, current_genid=current_genid,\n                    )\n\n            # Run meta agent\n            safe_log("Running meta agent...")\n            container_agentoutput_folder = os.path.join(\n                container_output_folder, "agent_output"\n            )\n            container_chat_history_file = os.path.join(\n                container_agentoutput_folder, "meta_agent_chat_history.md"\n            )\n            if run_baseline and "dgm" in run_baseline:\n                command = [\n                    "timeout",\n                    "21600",  # 6h timeout\n                    "python",\n                    "coding_agent.py",\n                    "--problem_statement",\n                    problem_statement,\n                    "--chat_history_file",\n                    container_chat_history_file,\n                    "--git_dir",\n                    f"/{REPO_NAME}",\n                    "--base_commit",\n                    commit_hash,\n                    "--outdir",\n                    container_agentoutput_folder,\n                ]\n            else:\n                command = [\n                    "timeout",\n                    "21600",  # 6h timeout\n                    "python",\n                    "run_meta_agent.py",\n                    "--chat_history_file",\n                    container_chat_history_file,\n                    "--repo_path",\n                    f"/{REPO_NAME}/",\n                    "--evals_folder",\n                    container_prev_eval_path,\n                    "--git_dir",\n                    f"/{REPO_NAME}",\n                    "--base_commit",\n                    commit_hash,\n                    "--outdir",\n                    container_agentoutput_folder,\n                    "--iterations_left",\n                    str(max_generation - current_genid),\n                    *(\n                        # If domain is polyglot, for a fair comparison with DGM\n                        ["--model", "claude-3-5-sonnet-20241022"] if domains == ["polyglot"] else []\n                    ),\n                ]\n\n            run_workdir = (\n                f"/DONOTTOUCH_{REPO_NAME}"\n                if run_baseline and "no_selfimprove" in run_baseline\n                else f"/{REPO_NAME}"\n            )\n            exec_result = container.exec_run(cmd=command, workdir=run_workdir)\n            log_container_output(exec_result)\n            metadata["parent_agent_success"] = exec_result.exit_code == 0\n\n            # Copy container outputs to local\n            local_agentoutput_folder = os.path.join(gen_output_dir, "agent_output/")\n            copy_from_container(\n                container,\n                source_path=container_agentoutput_folder,\n                dest_path=local_agentoutput_folder,\n            )\n\n            # Check if agent produced a diff\n            local_patch_file = os.path.join(\n                local_agentoutput_folder, "model_patch.diff"\n            )\n            metadata["curr_patch_files"].append(local_patch_file)\n            run_eval = file_exist_and_not_empty(local_patch_file)\n            metadata["run_eval"] = run_eval\n\n            # Run commands to check if the agents are compilable\n            run_commands_to_check_compilation(container, run_baseline=run_baseline, edit_select_parent=edit_select_parent)\n\n        # Evaluate the produced agent\n        if run_eval and "agent" in optimize_option:\n            log_path = os.path.join(gen_output_dir, "generate.log")\n\n            def eval_agent_worker(domain, eval_subset, eval_n):\n                setup_logger(log_path)  # Re-setup logger because of threading\n                eval_produced_agent(\n                    container,\n                    container_output_folder,\n                    gen_output_dir,\n                    domain=domain,\n                    eval_samples=eval_n,\n                    eval_workers=eval_workers,\n                    eval_subset=eval_subset,\n                    eval_test=eval_test,\n                )\n\n            # Small sample size evaluation for staged eval\n            if not skip_staged_eval:\n                stagedeval_samples = [\n                    get_domain_stagedeval_samples(domain) for domain in domains\n                ]\n                with ThreadPoolExecutor() as executor:\n                    futures = [\n                        executor.submit(eval_agent_worker, d, s, n)\n                        for d, s, n in zip(domains, eval_subsets, stagedeval_samples)\n                    ]\n                    try:\n                        for f in futures:\n                            f.result()\n                    except Exception as e:\n                        # Cancel all other futures if any job fails\n                        for future in futures:\n                            if not future.done():\n                                future.cancel()\n                        raise\n                stagedeval_scores = [\n                    get_score(domain, output_dir, current_genid) for domain in domains\n                ]\n                run_next_eval = all(\n                    [x is not None and x > 0 for x in stagedeval_scores]\n                )\n            else:\n                run_next_eval = True\n\n            # Full evaluation\n            if run_next_eval:\n                _per_domain_eval_samples = eval_samples\n                with ThreadPoolExecutor() as executor:\n                    futures = [\n                        executor.submit(eval_agent_worker, d, s, n)\n                        for d, s, n in zip(\n                            domains, eval_subsets, _per_domain_eval_samples\n                        )\n                    ]\n                    try:\n                        for f in futures:\n                            f.result()\n                    except Exception as e:\n                        # Cancel all other futures if any job fails\n                        for future in futures:\n                            if not future.done():\n                                future.cancel()\n                        raise\n                metadata["run_full_eval"] = True\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        safe_log(f"Error in generate: {e}")\n        metadata["run_eval"] = False\n\n    # Even on errors or KeyboardInterrupt\n    finally:\n        # Reset to the root commit\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(\n            cmd=["git", "reset", "--hard", root_commit], workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}"\n        )\n        log_container_output(exec_result)\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(\n            cmd=["git", "clean", "-fd"], workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}"\n        )\n        log_container_output(exec_result)\n\n        # Cleanup container\n        cleanup_container(container)\n\n        # Save metadata\n        eval_successful = all(\n            [\n                get_score(domain, output_dir, current_genid) is not None\n                for domain in domains\n            ]"\n    }\n    </json>'

agent ---\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport socket\nimport http.client\nimport time\n\n# Starting task agent (A_0) for the ARC-AGI-2 domain: one direct model response per attempt.\n# forward() makes two independent plain attempts (the budget is 2 model calls and 2 answers per\n# test input) and returns both parsed grids.\n\n\nclass _UnixConn(http.client.HTTPConnection):\n    def __init__(self, path, timeout=3600):\n        super().__init__("localhost", timeout=timeout)\n        self._path = path\n\n    def connect(self):\n        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)\n        s.settimeout(self.timeout)\n        s.connect(self._path)\n        self.sock = s\n\n\ndef call_model(prompt):\n    """One model call through the budget gateway (model and reasoning effort are fixed there)."""\n    path = os.environ.get("GW_SOCKET", "/run/arcgw/gw.sock")\n    body = json.dumps({"token": os.environ.get("GW_TOKEN", ""),\n                       "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]}).encode()\n    last = None\n    for attempt in range(3):\n        try:\n            c = _UnixConn(path)\n            c.request("POST", "/v1/chat", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})\n            r = c.getresponse()\n            data = json.loads(r.read() or b"{}")\n            c.close()\n            if r.status == 200:\n                return data.get("text", "")\n            last = f"gateway HTTP {r.status}: {data.get(\'error\')}"\n            if r.status in (402, 403, 429):\n                break\n        except Exception as e:\n            last = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"\n        time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\n    raise RuntimeError(last)\n\n\ndef grid_to_text(grid):\n    return "\\n".join(" ".join(str(v) for v in row) for row in grid)\n\n\ndef build_prompt(inputs):\n    parts = [\n        "You are solving an ARC-AGI puzzle. Each grid is a rectangular 2D array of integers 0-9 "\n        "(each integer is a color). The demonstration pairs below share one hidden transformation rule "\n        "that maps each input grid to its output grid. Infer the rule and apply it to the test input.",\n        "",\n    ]\n    for i, pair in enumerate(inputs["train"], 1):\n        parts.append(f"## Example {i}")\n        h, w = len(pair["input"]), len(pair["input"][0])\n        parts.append(f"Input ({h}x{w}):")\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["input"]))\n        h, w = len(pair["output"]), len(pair["output"][0])\n        parts.append(f"Output ({h}x{w}):")\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["output"]))\n        parts.append("")\n    t = inputs["test_input"]\n    parts.append("## Test")\n    parts.append(f"Input ({len(t)}x{len(t[0])}):")\n    parts.append(grid_to_text(t))\n    parts.append("")\n    parts.append(\n        "Infer one compact transformation rule from the demonstrations and check that it explains every "\n        "training pair, including object shapes, colors, positions, and grid dimensions. Apply that rule to "\n        "the test grid; do not assume its output has the same dimensions or layout as the input. Before "\n        "answering, check the proposed grid cell-by-cell and ensure it follows the rule. If multiple rules "\n        "fit, choose the simplest rule that explains all examples.\\n\\n"\n        "Give the output grid for the test input. End your response with the grid inside <answer></answer> "\n        "tags as a JSON list of rows, for example:\\n<answer>[[0, 1, 2], [3, 4, 5]]</answer>\\n"\n        "Put nothing else inside the tags."\n    )\n    return "\\n".join(parts)\n\n\ndef _valid(g):\n    return (isinstance(g, list) and len(g) > 0 and all(isinstance(r, list) and len(r) > 0 for r in g)\n            and len({len(r) for r in g}) == 1\n            and all(isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool) and 0 <= v <= 9 for r in g for v in r))\n\n\ndef _parse_block(s):\n    s = s.strip()\n    try:\n        g = json.loads(s)\n        if _valid(g):\n            return g\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    # rows of digits, optionally space/comma separated\n    rows = []\n    for line in s.splitlines():\n        line = line.strip().strip("[](),")\n        if not line:\n            continue\n        toks = re.findall(r"\\d", line) if re.fullmatch(r"[\\d\\s,]+", line) else None\n        if not toks:\n            return None\n        rows.append([int(x) for x in toks])\n    return rows if _valid(rows) else None\n\n\ndef parse_grid(text):\n    """Extract the answer grid from a model response; None if no valid grid is found."""\n    if not text:\n        return None\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"<answer>(.*?)</answer>", text, re.DOTALL)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"```(?:json)?\\s*(.*?)```", text, re.DOTALL)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"\\[\\s*\\[[\\d\\s,\\[\\]]*\\]\\s*\\]", text)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    return None\n\n\nclass TaskAgent:\n    def __init__(self, model="gpt-6-luna", chat_history_file="./chat_history.md"):\n        self.model = model\n        self.chat_history_file = chat_history_file\n\n    def log(self, msg):\n        try:\n            with open(self.chat_history_file, "a") as f:\n                f.write(msg + "\\n\\n")\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n    def solve_once(self, A grid is a rectangular list of rows of integers 0-9 (at most 30x30).\n\n## Task agent interface\n`task_agent.py` must define `TaskAgent(model=..., chat_history_file=...)` with\n`forward(inputs) -> (prediction, msg_history)`, called once per test input with\n`inputs = {"domain": "arc", "train": [...], "test_input": grid}`.\n`prediction` is a list of up to 2 candidate output grids (a single grid is also accepted).\n\n## Scoring\nExact match of the full output grid. Two answers count per test input: a test input is solved if\neither of the first two returned grids equals the true output. A task\'s score is the share of its\ntest inputs solved; a set\'s score (`overall_accuracy`) is the mean over tasks. Scoring happens\noutside the agent\'s environment; true test outputs are never available to agents.\n\n## Budget and environment (enforced outside the agent\'s code)\n* At most 2 model calls per test input (further calls are refused) and 2 answers per test input.\n  Model and reasoning effort are fixed by the gateway; call it as in `task_agent.py` / `agent/llm.py`.\n* No network. Each test input runs in a fresh process whose working directory `/tmp/task` holds only\n  that task\'s example pairs and the test input (`task.json`).\n* Model-written code can be executed with `from arc_sandbox import run_python` (no network, fresh\n  temp dir with `task.json`, 30 seconds wall clock per test input in total).\n* Visible tasks of the current set (example pairs + test inputs, no test outputs) are in\n  `domains/arc/data/tasks.json`. Evaluation results of earlier generations (per-task scores, wrong\n  answers, chat logs of unsolved test inputs) are in the generation folders of the evals folder.\n--- harness prediction scoring ---\nimport os\nimport sys\n\n# Add the project root to Python path\nsys.path.append(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))\n\nimport argparse\nimport importlib\nimport importlib.util\nimport os\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\nfrom datetime import datetime\nimport pandas as pd\nfrom hydra import compose, initialize_config_dir\nfrom types import ModuleType\n\n\ndef get_dataset(domain, subset=""):\n    df = None\n    if "imo_" in domain:\n        df = pd.read_csv(f"./domains/imo/{domain.split(\'_\')[-1]}bench{subset}.csv", dtype=str)\n    elif domain in ["search_arena", "paper_review"]:\n        df = pd.read_csv(f"./domains/{domain}/dataset{subset}.csv", dtype=str)\n    return df\n\ndef run_agent(TaskAgent, model, row, evals_folder, format_input_dict, question_id_col):\n    question_id = row[question_id_col]\n    chat_history_path = os.path.join(evals_folder, f"chat_history_{question_id}.md")\n    agent = TaskAgent(model=model, chat_history_file=chat_history_path)\n    inputs = format_input_dict(row)\n    prediction, _ = agent.forward(inputs)\n    return prediction\n\n\ndef load_task_agent(agent_path: str):\n    """\n    agent_path can be:\n      - a python file path: ./task_agent.py or /abs/path/task_agent.py\n      - a module path: proofgrader.task_agent or my_pkg.my_agent\n    Returns: TaskAgent class\n    """\n    # Case 1: looks like a file path or exists on disk\n    if agent_path.endswith(".py") or os.path.exists(agent_path):\n        abs_path = os.path.abspath(agent_path)\n        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("agent_module", abs_path)\n        if spec is None or spec.loader is None:\n            raise ImportError(f"Could not load spec from file: {abs_path}")\n        mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\n        spec.loader.exec_module(mod)\n        if not hasattr(mod, "TaskAgent"):\n            raise AttributeError(f"No TaskAgent found in file: {abs_path}")\n        return mod.TaskAgent\n\n    # Case 2: interpret as module path\n    mod = importlib.import_module(agent_path)\n    if not hasattr(mod, "TaskAgent"):\n        raise AttributeError(f"No TaskAgent found in module: {agent_path}")\n    return mod.TaskAgent\n\ndef harness(\n    agent_path="./task_agent.py",\n    output_dir="./outputs",\n    run_id=None,\n    domain="search_arena",\n    num_samples=-1,\n    save_interval=100,\n    num_workers=5,\n    resume_from=None,\n    subset="",\n    proofs_dname=None,\n):\n    # Dynamically import functions based on the domain\n    utils_prefix = domain.split("_", 1)[1] + "_" if domain.startswith("imo_") else ""\n    domain_folder = domain.split(\'_\')[0] if "imo_" in domain else domain\n    utils_module_path = f"domains.{domain_folder}.{utils_prefix}utils"\n    utils_module = importlib.import_module(utils_module_path)\n    format_input_dict = utils_module.format_input_dict\n    question_id_col = utils_module.QUESTION_ID\n    model = utils_module.MODEL\n\n    # Load TaskAgent either from a file path or an importable module path\n    TaskAgent = load_task_agent(agent_path)\n\n    # Specify output folder\n    if resume_from:\n        output_folder = os.path.abspath(resume_from)\n    else:\n        run_id = (\n            datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f") if run_id is None else run_id\n        )\n        output_folder = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), output_dir, run_id)\n\n    # Create output folder\n    evals_folder = os.path.join(output_folder, "agent_evals")\n    os.makedirs(evals_folder, exist_ok=True)\n    output_path = os.path.join(output_folder, "predictions.csv")\n\n    # Load existing predictions if available\n    if os.path.exists(output_path):\n        existing_df = pd.read_csv(output_path, dtype=str)\n        completed_ids = set(\n            existing_df[~existing_df["prediction"].isna()][question_id_col]\n        )\n    else:\n        existing_df = None\n        completed_ids = set()\n\n    # Get dataset\n    if proofs_dname:\n        dataset = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(proofs_dname, "predictions.csv"), dtype=str)\n        dataset["Response"] = dataset["prediction"].copy()\n        dataset.drop(columns=["prediction"], inplace=True)\n    else:\n        dataset = get_dataset(domain=domain, subset=subset)\n    if num_samples > 0:\n        dataset = dataset[:num_samples]\n\n    # Add a prediction column\n    if existing_df is not None:\n        dataset = dataset.merge(\n            existing_df[[question_id_col, "prediction"]], on=question_id_col, how="left"\n        )\n    else:\n        dataset["prediction"] = None\n\n    predictions = dataset["prediction"].tolist()\n    futures = []\n\n    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\n        for i, row in dataset.iterrows():\n            if (\n                pd.notna(row["prediction"]) or row[question_id_col] in completed_ids\n            ):  # pyright: ignore\n                continue\n            futures.append(\n                (\n                    i,\n                    executor.submit(\n                        run_agent,\n                        TaskAgent, model, row, evals_folder,\n                        format_input_dict, question_id_col,\n                    ),\n                )\n            )\n\n        for idx, future in futures:\n            prediction = future.result()\n            predictions[idx] = prediction\n\n            if (idx + 1) % save_interval == 0:\n                dataset["prediction"] = predictions\n                dataset.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n                print(f"Checkpoint saved to {output_path}")\n\n    # Final save\n    dataset["prediction"] = predictions\n    dataset.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\n    print(f"Final predictions saved to {output_path}")\n\n    return output_folder\n\n\nif __name__ == "__main__":\n    # Parse command-line arguments\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description="Evaluate a system on the search arena dataset."\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--agent_path", type=str, default="./task_agent.py", help="Path to the agent"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--output_dir", type=str, default="./outputs", help="Output directory"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument("--run_id", type=str, default=None, help="Run ID")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        "--domain",\n        type=str,\n        choices=[\n            "search_arena",\n            "paper_review",\n            "balrog_babyai",\n            "balrog_babaisai",\n            "balrog_minihack",\n            "balrog_nle",\n            "genesis_go2walking",\n            "genesis_go2walkback",\n            "genesis_go2hop",\n            "imo_grading",\n            "imo_proof",\n            "imo_proof_grading",  #
