setup_logger(os.path.join(generate_output_dir, "select_next_parent.log"))\\n\\n    # Get the latest node that can select parent\\n    latest_node = get_latest_can_select_parent(archive, generate_output_dir)\\n    safe_log(f"select_next_parent_container: latest_node={latest_node}")\\n    prev_patch_files = get_patch_files(generate_output_dir, latest_node)\\n\\n    # Create and start the Docker container\\n    image_name = f"{REPO_NAME}"\\n    run_id = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")\\n    container_name = f"{REPO_NAME}-nextparent-container-{run_id}"\\n    container = build_container(docker_client, root_dir, image_name, container_name, verbose=False)\\n    container.start()\\n    container_output_folder = "/tmp/"\\n\\n    try:\\n        # Apply all lineage diffs\\n        commit_hash = apply_diffs_container(container, prev_patch_files, verbose=False)\\n\\n        # Copy generate_output_dir to container\\n        container_generate_output_dir = os.path.join(\\n            container_output_folder, generate_output_dir.split(os.sep)[-1]\\n        )\\n        copy_to_container(\\n            container,\\n            source_path=generate_output_dir,\\n            dest_path=container_generate_output_dir,\\n            verbose=False,\\n        )\\n\\n        # Get next parent in container\\n        command = [\\n            "timeout",\\n            "3600",  # 1h timeout\\n            "python",\\n            "-m",\\n            "utils.run_select_next_parent",\\n            "--domains",\\n            *domains,\\n            "--generate_output_dir",\\n            container_generate_output_dir,\\n        ]\\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(cmd=command, workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}")\\n        log_container_output(exec_result, verbose=True)\\n\\n        # Get next parent outputs\\n        container_output_strings = exec_result.output.decode().strip().split("\\\\n")\\n        next_parent_genid = container_output_strings[-1]\\n        next_parent_genid = int(next_parent_genid) if not is_starting_node(next_parent_genid) else next_parent_genid\\n\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        safe_log(f"Error in select_next_parent_container: {e}")\\n        update_node_metadata(generate_output_dir, latest_node, {"can_select_next_parent": False})\\n        next_parent_genid = None\\n\\n    # Even on errors or KeyboardInterrupt\\n    finally:\\n        # Reset to the root commit\\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(\\n            cmd=["git", "reset", "--hard", root_commit], workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}"\\n        )\\n        log_container_output(exec_result, verbose=False)\\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(\\n            cmd=["git", "clean", "-fd"], workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}"\\n        )\\n        log_container_output(exec_result, verbose=False)\\n\\n        # Cleanup container\\n        cleanup_container(container, verbose=False)\\n\\n    # Try again if no parent is selected\\n    if next_parent_genid is None:\\n        if max_attempts > 0:\\n            next_parent_genid = select_next_parent_container(\\n                docker_client,\\n                domains,\\n                generate_output_dir,\\n                archive,\\n                root_dir,\\n                root_commit,\\n                max_attempts=max_attempts - 1,\\n            )\\n        else:\\n            # In case of infinite recursive, but it should not happen\\n            raise Exception("Max attempts reached in select_next_parent_container")\\n\\n    return next_parent_genid\\n\\ndef get_ensemble_scores_container(\\n    docker_client,\\n    domain,\\n    generate_output_dir,\\n    gen_output_dir,\\n    root_dir="./",\\n    root_commit="HEAD",\\n    prev_patch_files=[],\\n    num_samples=-1,\\n    max_workers=5,\\n    subsets=[],\\n):\\n    # Setup logger\\n    logger = setup_logger(os.path.join(gen_output_dir, "ensemble.log"))\\n\\n    # Create and start the Docker container\\n    image_name = f"{REPO_NAME}"\\n    run_id = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")\\n    container_name = f"{REPO_NAME}-ens-container-{run_id}"\\n    container = build_container(docker_client, root_dir, image_name, container_name)\\n    container.start()\\n    container_output_folder = "/tmp/"\\n\\n    try:\\n        # Apply all lineage diffs\\n        commit_hash = apply_diffs_container(container, prev_patch_files)\\n\\n        # Copy generate_output_dir to container\\n        container_generate_output_dir = os.path.join(\\n            container_output_folder, generate_output_dir.split(os.sep)[-1]\\n        )\\n        copy_to_container(\\n            container,\\n            source_path=generate_output_dir,\\n            dest_path=container_generate_output_dir,\\n        )\\n\\n        # Get ensemble scores in container\\n        scores = []\\n        for subset in subsets:\\n            command = [\\n                "timeout",\\n                "10800",  # 3h timeout\\n                "python",\\n                "-m",\\n                "utils.run_ensemble",\\n                "--domain",\\n                domain,\\n                "--generate_output_dir",\\n                container_generate_output_dir,\\n                "--num_samples",\\n                str(num_samples),\\n                "--max_workers",\\n                str(max_workers),\\n                "--subset",\\n                subset,\\n            ]\\n            exec_result = container.exec_run(cmd=command, workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}")\\n            log_container_output(exec_result)\\n\\n            # Get ensemble outputs\\n            container_output_strings = exec_result.output.decode().strip().split("\\\\n")\\n            score = float(container_output_strings[-3])\\n            scores.append(score)\\n===== meta_agent.py =====\\n# Copyright (c) Meta Platforms, Inc. and affiliates.\\n\\nfrom agent.base_agent import AgentSystem\\nfrom agent.llm_withtools import chat_with_agent\\n\\nclass MetaAgent(AgentSystem):\\n    def forward(self, repo_path, eval_path, iterations_left=None):\\n        """\\n        A meta agent that recursively self-improves.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            repo_path (str): The path to the repository.\\n            eval_path (str): The path to previously generated agents and their evaluation results.\\n            iterations_left (int, optional): The number of remaining iterations in which the meta agent will be invoked in future. Defaults to None.\\n        """\\n        instruction = f"Modify any part of the codebase at `{repo_path}`."\\n        # [experiment adaptation, see DECISIONS.md] gpt-6-luna otherwise asks for clarification and never edits.\\n        instruction += (\\n            f"\\\\n\\\\nThe repository\\\'s README.md describes the goal. Previously generated agents and their evaluation results are at `{eval_path}`. "\\n            "Work autonomously: nobody will answer questions, so do not ask for clarification. "\\n            "Use the tools to inspect the code and make your changes; a response without a tool call ends the session, so only reply without one when you are done."\\n        )\\n\\n        new_msg_history = chat_with_agent(instruction, model=self.model, msg_history=[], logging=self.log, tools_available=\\\'all\\\')\\n===== task_agent.py =====\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport re\\nimport socket\\nimport http.client\\nimport time\\n\\n# Starting task agent (A_0) for the ARC-AGI-2 domain: one direct model response per attempt.\\n# forward() makes two independent plain attempts (the budget is 2 model calls and 2 answers per\\n# test input) and returns both parsed grids.\\n\\n\\nclass _UnixConn(http.client.HTTPConnection):\\n    def __init__(self, path, timeout=3600):\\n        super().__init__("localhost", timeout=timeout)\\n        self._path = path\\n\\n    def connect(self):\\n        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)\\n        s.settimeout(self.timeout)\\n        s.connect(self._path)\\n        self.sock = s\\n\\n\\ndef call_model(prompt):\\n    """One model call through the budget gateway (model and reasoning effort are fixed there)."""\\n    path = os.environ.get("GW_SOCKET", "/run/arcgw/gw.sock")\\n    body = json.dumps({"token": os.environ.get("GW_TOKEN", ""),\\n                       "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]}).encode()\\n    last = None\\n    for attempt in range(3):\\n        try:\\n            c = _UnixConn(path)\\n            c.request("POST", "/v1/chat", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})\\n            r = c.getresponse()\\n            data = json.loads(r.read() or b"{}")\\n            c.close()\\n            if r.status == 200:\\n                return data.get("text", "")\\n            last = f"gateway HTTP {r.status}: {data.get(\\\'error\\\')}"\\n            if r.status in (402, 403, 429):\\n                break\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            last = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"\\n        time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\\n    raise RuntimeError(last)\\n\\n\\ndef grid_to_text(grid):\\n    return "\\\\n".join(" ".join(str(v) for v in row) for row in grid)\\n\\n\\ndef build_prompt(inputs):\\n    parts = [\\n        "You are solving an ARC-AGI puzzle. Each grid is a rectangular 2D array of integers 0-9 "\\n        "(each integer is a color). The demonstration pairs below share one hidden transformation rule "\\n        "that maps each input grid to its output grid. Infer the rule and apply it to the test input.",\\n        "",\\n    ]\\n    for i, pair in enumerate(inputs["train"], 1):\\n        parts.append(f"## Example {i}")\\n        h, w = len(pair["input"]), len(pair["input"][0])\\n        parts.append(f"Input ({h}x{w}):")\\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["input"]))\\n        h, w = len(pair["output"]), len(pair["output"][0])\\n        parts.append(f"Output ({h}x{w}):")\\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["output"]))\\n        parts.append("")\\n    t = inputs["test_input"]\\n    parts.append("## Test")\\n    parts.append(f"Input ({len(t)}x{len(t[0])}):")\\n    parts.append(grid_to_text(t))\\n    parts.append("")\\n    parts.append(\\n        "Give the output grid for the test input. End your response with the grid inside <answer></answer> "\\n        "tags as a JSON list of rows, for example:\\\\n<answer>[[0, 1, 2], [3, 4, 5]]</answer>\\\\n"\\n        "Put nothing else inside the tags."\\n    )\\n    return "\\\\n".join(parts)\\n\\n\\ndef _valid(g):\\n    return (isinstance(g, list) and len(g) > 0 and all(isinstance(r, list) and len(r) > 0 for r in g)\\n            and len({len(r) for r in g}) == 1\\n            and all(isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool) and 0 <= v <= 9 for r in g for v in r))\\n\\n\\ndef _parse_block(s):\\n    s = s.strip()\\n    try:\\n        g = json.loads(s)\\n        if _valid(g):\\n            return g\\n    except Exception:\\n        pass\\n    # rows of digits, optionally space/comma separated\\n    rows = []\\n    for line in s.splitlines():\\n        line = line.strip().strip("[](),")\\n        if not line:\\n            continue\\n        toks = re.findall(r"\\\\d", line) if re.fullmatch(r"[\\\\d\\\\s,]+", line) else None\\n        if not toks:\\n            return None\\n        rows.append([int(x) for x in toks])\\n    return rows if _valid(rows) else None\\n\\n\\ndef parse_grid(text):\\n    """Extract the answer grid from a model response; None if no valid grid is found."""\\n    if not text:\\n        return None\\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"<answer>(.*?)</answer>", text, re.DOTALL)):\\n        g = _parse_block(m)\\n        if g is not None:\\n            return g\\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"```(?:json)?\\\\s*(.*?)```", text, re.DOTALL)):\\n        g = _parse_block(m)\\n        if g is not None:\\n            return g\\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"\\\\[\\\\s*\\\\[[\\\\d\\\\s,\\\\[\\\\]]*\\\\]\\\\s*\\\\]", text)):\\n        g = _parse_block(m)\\n        if g is not None:\\n            return g\\n    return None\\n\\n\\nclass TaskAgent:\\n    def __init__(self, model="gpt-6-luna", chat_history_file="./chat_history.md"):\\n        self.model = model\\n        self.chat_history_file = chat_history_file\\n\\n    def log(self, msg):\\n        try:\\n            with open(self.chat_history_file, "a") as f:\\n                f.write(msg + "\\\\n\\\\n")\\n        except Exception:\\n            pass\\n\\n    def solve_once(self, inputs):\\n        """One direct model response -> one parsed grid (or None)."""\\n        prompt = build_prompt(inputs)\\n        self.log(f"Input: {prompt}")\\n        text = call_model(prompt)\\n        self.log(f"Output: {text}")\\n        return parse_grid(text)\\n\\n    def forward(self, inputs):\\n        """\\n        Args:\\n            inputs (dict): {"domain": "arc", "train": [{"input": grid, "output": grid}, ...], "test_input": grid}\\n        Returns:\\n            tuple: (prediction, msg_history) where prediction is a list of up to 2 candidate grids.\\n        """\\n        answers = []\\n        for _ in range(2):\\n            try:\\n                g = self.solve_once(inputs)\\n            except Exception as e:\\n                self.log(f"Error: {e}")\\n                g = None\\n            if g is not None:\\n                answers.append(g)\\n        return answers, []\\n===== run_meta_agent.py =====\\nimport argparse\\nimport os\\n\\nfrom agent.llm import CLAUDE_MODEL\\nfrom meta_agent import MetaAgent\\nfrom utils.git_utils import diff_versus_commit, reset_paths_to_commit\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    # Parse command-line arguments\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--model",\\n        type=str,\\n        default=CLAUDE_MODEL,\\n        help="Model to use for the agent",\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--chat_history_file",\\n        type=str,\\n        default="./outputs/chat_history.md",\\n        help="Path to chat history file",\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--repo_path", type=str, default="./", help="Path to the agent file"\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--evals_folder",\\n        type=str,\\n        default="./outputs/",\\n        help="Path to the folder containing the evaluation files",\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--iterations_left",\\n        type=int,\\n        default=None,\\n        help="The number of remaining iterations in which the meta agent will be invoked in future.",\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--git_dir", required=True, help="Path to git repository directory"\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--base_commit", required=True, help="Base commit hash to compare against"\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--outdir", required=False, default="./outputs/", help="Output directory"\\n    )\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    # Run meta agent\\n    meta_agent = MetaAgent(\\n        model=args.model,\\n        chat_history_file=args.chat_history_file,\\n    )\\n    meta_agent.forward(\\n        repo_path=args.repo_path,\\n        eval_path=args.evals_folder,\\n        iterations_left=args.iterations_left,\\n    )\\n\\n    # Reset unwanted diffs\\n    reset_paths_to_commit(\\n        git_dname=args.git_dir, commit=args.base_commit, paths=["domains/"]\\n    )\\n\\n    # Save git diff\\n    model_patch = diff_versus_commit(args.git_dir, args.base_commit)\\n    model_patch_outfile = (\\n        os.path.join(args.outdir, "model_patch.diff")\\n        if args.outdir\\n        else "model_patch.diff"\\n    )\\n    with open(model_patch_outfile, "w") as f:\\n        f.write(model_patch)\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == "__main__":\\n    main()\\n===== agent/base_agent.py =====\\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\\nfrom agent.llm import OPENAI_MODEL\\nfrom utils.thread_logger import ThreadLoggerManager\\n\\n\\nclass AgentSystem(ABC):\\n    def __init__(\\n        self,\\n        model=OPENAI_MODEL,\\n        chat_history_file=\\\'./outputs/chat_history.md\\\',\\n    ):\\n        self.model = model\\n\\n        # Initialize logger and store it in thread-local storage\\n        self.logger_manager = ThreadLoggerManager(log_file=chat_history_file)\\n        self.log = self.logger_manager.log\\n\\n        # Clear the log file\\n        with open(chat_history_file, \\\'w\\\') as f:\\n            f.write(\\\'\\\')\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def forward(self, *args, **kwargs):\\n        pass\\n===== agent/llm.py =====\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport socket\\nimport http.client\\nimport time\\nfrom typing import Tuple\\n\\n# NOTE (experiment infrastructure): every model call goes through the budget gateway at the unix\\n# socket $GW_SOCKET using the token in $GW_TOKEN. The gateway fixes the model (gpt-6-luna) and the\\n# reasoning effort for the caller\\\'s role, and enforces the per-test-input / per-iteration budgets.\\n# The `model`, `temperature` and `max_tokens` arguments below are accepted for compatibility only.\\n\\nMAX_TOKENS = 16384\\n\\nCLAUDE_MODEL = "gpt-6-luna"\\nCLAUDE_HAIKU_MODEL = "gpt-6-luna"\\nCLAUDE_35NEW_MODEL = "gpt-6-luna"\\nOPENAI_MODEL = "gpt-6-luna"\\nOPENAI_MINI_MODEL = "gpt-6-luna"\\nOPENAI_O3_MODEL = "gpt-6-luna"\\nOPENAI_O3MINI_MODEL = "gpt-6-luna"\\nOPENAI_O4MINI_MODEL = "gpt-6-luna"\\nOPENAI_GPT52_MODEL = "gpt-6-luna"\\nOPENAI_GPT5_MODEL = "gpt-6-luna"\\nOPENAI_GPT5MINI_MODEL = "gpt-6-luna"\\nGEMINI_3_MODEL = "gpt-6-luna"\\nGEMINI_MODEL = "gpt-6-luna"\\nGEMINI_FLASH_MODEL = "gpt-6-luna"\\n\\n\\nclass _UnixConn(http.client.HTTPConnection):\\n    def __init__(self, path, timeout=3600):\\n        super().__init__("localhost", timeout=timeout)\\n        self._path = path\\n\\n    def connect(self):\\n        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)\\n        s.settimeout(self.timeout)\\n        s.connect(self._path)\\n        self.sock = s\\n\\n\\ndef _gateway_chat(messages):\\n    path = os.environ.get("GW_SOCKET", "/run/arcgw/gw.sock")\\n    token = os.environ.get("GW_TOKEN", "")\\n    body = json.dumps({"token": token, "messages": messages}).encode()\\n    last = None\\n    for attempt in range(4):\\n        try:\\n            c = _UnixConn(path)\\n            c.request("POST", "/v1/chat", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})\\n            r = c.getresponse()\\n            data = json.loads(r.read() or b"{}")\\n            c.close()\\n            if r.status == 200:\\n                return data.get("text", "")\\n            last = f"gateway HTTP {r.status}: {data.get(\\\'error\\\')}"\\n            if r.status in (402, 403, 429):\\n                break  # budget refusals are final\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            last = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"\\n        time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\\n    raise RuntimeError(last)\\n\\n\\ndef get_response_from_llm(\\n    msg: str,\\n    model: str = OPENAI_MODEL,\\n    temperature: float = 0.0,\\n    max_tokens: int = MAX_TOKENS,\\n    msg_history=None,\\n) -> Tuple[str, list, dict]:\\n    if msg_history is None:\\n        msg_history = []\\n\\n    # Convert text to content\\n    msg_history = [\\n        {**m, "content": m.pop("text")} if "text" in m else m\\n        for m in msg_history\\n    ]\\n\\n    new_msg_history = msg_history + [{"role": "user", "content": msg}]\\n\\n    response_text = _gateway_chat([{"role": m["role"], "content": m["content"]} for m in new_msg_history])\\n    new_msg_history.append({"role": "assistant", "content": response_text})\\n\\n    # Convert content to text, compatible with MetaGen API\\n    new_msg_history = [\\n        {**m, "text": m.pop("content")} if "content" in m else m\\n        for m in new_msg_history\\n    ]\\n\\n    return response_text, new_msg_history, {}\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == "__main__":\\n    print(get_response_from_llm("Hello there!")[0])\\n===== agent/llm_withtools.py =====\\nimport re\\nimport json\\n\\nfrom agent.llm import get_response_from_llm\\nfrom agent.tools import load_tools\\n\\ndef get_tooluse_prompt(tool_infos=[]):\\n    """\\n    Get the prompt for using the available tools.\\n    """\\n    # If no tools are available, return an empty string\\n    if not tool_infos or len(tool_infos) == 0:\\n        return ""\\n    # Create the prompt\\n    tools_available = [str(tool_info) for tool_info in tool_infos]\\n    tools_available = \\\'\\\\n\\\\n\\\'.join(tools_available) if tools_available else \\\'None\\\'\\n    tooluse_prompt = """Here are the available tools:\\n```\\n{tools_available}\\n```\\n\\nUse only one tool (if needed) in this format:\\n<json>\\n{{\\n    "tool_name": ...,\\n    "tool_input": ...\\n}}\\n</json>\\n\\nONLY USE ONE TOOL PER RESPONSE, AND STRICTLY FOLLOW THE FORMAT OF TOOL_NAME AND TOOL_INPUT ABOVE.\\nDO NOT HALLUCINATE OR MAKE UP ANYTHING.\\n""".format(tools_available=tools_available)\\n    return tooluse_prompt.strip()\\n\\ndef should_retry_tool_use(response, tool_uses=None):\\n    """\\n    Check if the response attempts to use a tool,\\n    but ran out of output context.\\n    """\\n    # If there are tool uses, we don\\\'t need to check for retry\\n    if tool_uses is not None and len(tool_uses) > 0:\\n        return False\\n\\n    # Find positions of the markers\\n    json_pos = response.find("<json>")\\n    tool_name_pos = response.find("tool_name")\\n    tool_input_pos = response.find("tool_input")\\n\\n    # Check ordering and length condition\\n    if (\\n        json_pos != -1\\n        and tool_name_pos != -1\\n        and tool_input_pos != -1\\n        and json_pos < tool_name_pos < tool_input_pos\\n        and len(response) >= 2000\\n    ):\\n        return True\\n\\n    # No retry\\n    return False\\n\\ndef check_for_tool_uses(response):\\n    """\\n    Checks if the response contains one or more tool calls in json code blocks.\\n    Returns a list of tool use dictionaries.\\n    """\\n    pattern = r\\\'<json>\\\\s*(\\\\{.*?\\\\})\\\\s*</json>\\\'\\n    matches = re.findall(pattern, response, re.DOTALL)\\n    tool_uses = []\\n\\n    for match in matches:\\n        try:\\n            tool_use = json.loads(match)\\n            if \\\'tool_name\\\' not in tool_use or \\\'tool_input\\\' not in tool_use:\\n                continue  # Skip invalid tool use\\n            tool_uses.append(tool_use)\\n        except json.JSONDecodeError:\\n            continue  # Skip malformed JSON blocks\\n\\n    # [experiment adaptation, see DECISIONS.md] also accept a bare JSON tool call without <json> tags\\n    if not tool_uses:\\n        dec = json.JSONDecoder()\\n        i = response.find(\\\'{\\\')\\n        while i != -1:\\n            try:\\n                obj, end = dec.raw_decode(response, i)\\n                if isinstance(obj, dict) and \\\'tool_name\\\' in obj and \\\'tool_input\\\' in obj:\\n                    tool_uses.append(obj)\\n                    break\\n            except json.JSONDecodeError:\\n                pass\\n            i = response.find(\\\'{\\\', i + 1)\\n\\n    return tool_uses if tool_uses else None\\n\\ndef process_tool_call(tools_dict, tool_name, tool_input):\\n    try:\\n        if tool_name in tools_dict:\\n            return tools_dict[tool_name][\\\'function\\\'](**tool_input)\\n        else:\\n            return f"Error: Tool \\\'{tool_name}\\\' not found"\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        return f"Error executing tool \\\'{tool_name}\\\': {str(e)}"\\n\\ndef chat_with_agent(\\n    msg,\\n    model="claude-4-sonnet-genai",\\n    msg_history=None,\\n    logging=print,\\n    tools_available=[],  # Empty list means no tools, \\\'all\\\' means all tools\\n    multiple_tool_calls=False,  # Whether to allow multiple tool calls in a single response\\n    max_tool_calls=40,  # Maximum number of tool calls allowed in a single response, -1 for unlimited\\n):\\n    get_response_fn = get_response_from_llm\\n    # Construct message\\n    if msg_history is None:\\n        msg_history = []\\n    new_msg_history = msg_history\\n\\n    try:\\n        # Load all tools\\n        all_tools = load_tools(logging=logging, names=tools_available)\\n        tools_dict = {tool[\\\'info\\\'][\\\'name\\\']: tool for tool in all_tools}\\n        system_msg = f"{get_tooluse_prompt([tool[\\\'info\\\'] for tool in all_tools])}\\\\n\\\\n"\\n        num_tool_calls = 0\\n\\n        # Call API\\n        logging(f"Input: {repr(msg)}")\\n        response, new_msg_history, info = get_response_fn(\\n            msg=system_msg + msg,\\n            model=model,\\n            msg_history=new_msg_history,\\n        )\\n        logging(f"Output: {repr(response)}")\\n        # logging(f"Info: {repr(info)}")\\n\\n        # Tool use\\n        tool_uses = check_for_tool_uses(response)\\n        retry_tool_use = should_retry_tool_use(response, tool_uses)\\n        num_nudges = 0\\n        while True:\\n            # [experiment adaptation, see DECISIONS.md] gpt-6-luna sometimes ends a turn after only\\n            # announcing what it will do; nudge (at most 2 times in a row) before treating it as finished.\\n            if not (tool_uses or retry_tool_use):\\n                if num_nudges >= 2 or \\\'DONE\\\' in response or (max_tool_calls > 0 and num_tool_calls >= max_tool_calls):\\n                    break\\n                num_nudges += 1\\n                nudge = ("No tool call was found in your last response. If you are not finished, make the next tool call now, "\\n                         "using exactly the <json> format above. If you are finished, reply with the single word DONE.")\\n                logging(f"Nudge: {repr(nudge)}")\\n                response, new_msg_history, info = get_response_fn(\\n                    msg=nudge, model=model, msg_history=new_msg_history,\\n                )\\n                logging(f"Output: {repr(response)}")\\n                tool_uses = check_for_tool_uses(response)\\n                retry_tool_use = should_retry_tool_use(response, tool_uses)\\n                continue\\n            num_nudges = 0\\n            # Check for max tool calls\\n            if max_tool_calls > 0 and num_tool_calls >= max_tool_calls:\\n                logging("Error: Maximum number of tool calls reached.")\\n                break\\n\\n            tool_msgs = []\\n\\n            # Process tool uses\\n            if tool_uses:\\n                tool_uses = tool_uses if multiple_tool_calls else tool_uses[:1]\\n                for tool_use in tool_uses:\\n                    tool_name = tool_use[\\\'tool_name\\\']\\n                    tool_input = tool_use[\\\'tool_input\\\']\\n                    tool_output = process_tool_call(tools_dict, tool_name, tool_input)\\n                    num_tool_calls += 1\\n                    tool_msg = f\\\'\\\'\\\'<json>\\n    {{\\n        "tool_name": "{tool_name}",\\n        "tool_input": {tool_input},\\n        "tool_output": "{tool_output}"\\n    }}\\n    </json>\\\'\\\'\\\'.strip()\\n                    logging(f"Tool output: {repr(tool_msg)}")\\n                    tool_msgs.append(tool_msg)\\n\\n            # Check for retry\\n            if retry_tool_use:\\n                logging("Error: Output context exceeded. Please try again.")\\n                tool_msgs.append("Error: Output context exceeded. Please try again.")\\n\\n            # Get tool response\\n            response, new_msg_history, info = get_response_fn(\\n                msg=system_msg + \\\'\\\\n\\\\n\\\'.join(tool_msgs),\\n                model=model,\\n                msg_history=new_msg_history,\\n            )\\n            logging(f"Output: {repr(response)}")\\n            # logging(f"Info: {repr(info)}")\\n\\n            # Check for next tool use\\n            tool_uses = check_for_tool_uses(response)\\n            retry_tool_use = should_retry_tool_use(response, tool_uses)\\n\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        logging(f"Error: {str(e)}")\\n        raise e\\n\\n    return new_msg_history\\n\\nif __name__ == "__main__":\\n    msg = """hello"""\\n    new_msg_history = chat_with_agent(msg)\\n===== domains/run_eval.py =====\\nimport argparse\\nimport os\\nfrom datetime import datetime\\nimport shutil\\nimport docker\\n\\nfrom utils.constants import REPO_NAME\\nfrom utils.docker_utils import (\\n    build_container,\\n    cleanup_container,\\n    copy_from_container,\\n    log_container_output,\\n    safe_log,\\n    setup_logger,\\n)\\nfrom utils.domain_utils import get_domain_test_subset\\nfrom utils.gl_utils import apply_diffs_container, setup_initial_gen\\n\\n\\ndef run_eval(\\n    output_dir,\\n    domain,\\n    run_id=None,\\n    num_samples=-1,\\n    num_workers=5,\\n    subset="",\\n    patch_files=None,\\n    copy_root_dir=None,\\n):\\n    # Setup\\n    parent_output_dir = (\\n        os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "outputs/") if output_dir is None else output_dir\\n    )\\n    output_dir = os.path.join(parent_output_dir, f"{run_id}/")\\n    if os.path.exists(output_dir):\\n        # If output_dir already exists, it means that the evaluation was already done, so skip\\n        print(f"Output directory {output_dir} already exists, skipping.")\\n        return\\n    root_dir, root_commit = setup_initial_gen(\\n        output_dir, [domain], copy_root_dir=copy_root_dir,\\n        subsets=[subset], resume=False, copy_eval=False,\\n    )\\n    logger = setup_logger(os.path.join(output_dir, "run_eval.log"))\\n\\n    # Save args\\n    args_dict = locals()\\n    args_str = ", ".join([f"{k}={v}" for k, v in args_dict.items()])\\n    safe_log(f"Args: {args_str}\\\\n")\\n\\n    # Create and start the Docker container\\n    image_name = f"{REPO_NAME}"\\n    container_name = f"{REPO_NAME}-{domain}-eval-container-{run_id}"\\n    client = docker.DockerClient()\\n    container = build_container(\\n        client, root_dir, image_name, container_name, domains=[domain],\\n    )\\n\\n    container.start()\\n    container_output_folder = "/tmp/"\\n\\n    try:\\n        # Apply diffs if specified\\n        patch_files = patch_files if patch_files is not None else []\\n        commit_hash = apply_diffs_container(container, patch_files)\\n\\n        # Run harness and report\\n        safe_log("Evaluating the agent...")\\n        eval_run_id = f"{domain}_eval"\\n        container_evaloutput_folder = os.path.join(container_output_folder, eval_run_id)\\n        command = [\\n            "timeout",\\n            "21600",  # 6h timeout\\n            "python",\\n            "-m",\\n            f"domains.harness",\\n            "--output_dir",\\n            container_output_folder,\\n            "--run_id",\\n            eval_run_id,\\n            "--domain",\\n            domain,\\n            "--num_samples",\\n            str(num_samples),\\n            "--num_workers",\\n            str(num_workers),\\n            "--subset",\\n            subset,\\n        ]\\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(cmd=command, workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}")\\n        log_container_output(exec_result)\\n        command = [\\n            "timeout",\\n            "3600",  # 1h timeout\\n            "python",\\n            "-m",\\n            f"domains.report",\\n            "--domain",\\n            domain,\\n            "--dname",\\n            os.path.join(container_output_folder, eval_run_id),\\n        ]\\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(cmd=command, workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}")\\n        log_container_output(exec_result)\\n        # Copy container outputs to local, evaluation results\\n        evaloutput_folder = os.path.join(output_dir, f"{domain}/")\\n        copy_from_container(\\n            container,\\n            source_path=container_evaloutput_folder,\\n            dest_path=evaloutput_folder,\\n        )\\n\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        print(f"Error in run_eval: {e}")\\n        pass\\n\\n    # Even on errors or KeyboardInterrupt\\n    finally:\\n        # Reset to the root commit (only if container is still running)\\n        try:\\n            container.reload()\\n            exec_result = container.exec_run(\\n                cmd=["git", "reset", "--hard", root_commit], workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}"\\n            )\\n            log_container_output(exec_result)\\n            exec_result = container.exec_run(\\n                cmd=["git", "clean", "-fd"], workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}"\\n            )\\n            log_container_output(exec_result)\\n        except Exception as cleanup_error:\\n            print(f"Warning: Could not reset git in container: {cleanup_error}")\\n\\n        # Cleanup container\\n        cleanup_container(container)\\n\\n        # Remove root dir from local\\n        shutil.rmtree(os.path.dirname(root_dir))\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == "__main__":\\n    # Parse command-line arguments\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\\n        description="Evaluate a system on the TEST subsets."\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--output_dir", type=str, default="./outputs", help="Output directory"\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument("--run_id", type=str, default=None, help="Run ID")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--domains",\\n        type=str,\\n        nargs="+",  # one or more domains\\n        required=True,\\n        help="One or more domains to evaluate (must be from the allowed list)",\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--num_samples",\\n        type=int,\\n        default=-1,\\n        help="Number of samples to evaluate, -1 for all",\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--num_workers", type=int, default=10, help="Number of parallel workers"\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--patch_files", type=str, nargs="+", default=None, help="Patch files to apply"\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--repeat", type=int, default=1, help="Number of times to repeat this eval"\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--copy_root_dir", type=str, default=None, help="Path to a root dir to copy"\\n    )\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    base_run_id = args.run_id\\n    base_run_id = (\\n        datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")\\n        if base_run_id is None\\n        else base_run_id\\n    )\\n    test_subsets = [get_domain_test_subset(d) for d in args.domains]\\n\\n    for domain, subset in zip(args.domains, test_subsets):\\n        for i in range(args.repeat):\\n            run_id = f"{base_run_id}_{domain}_{i}"\\n            run_eval(\\n                output_dir=args.output_dir,\\n                domain=domain,\\n                run_id=run_id,\\n                num_samples=args.num_samples,\\n                num_workers=args.num_workers,\\n                subset=subset,\\n                patch_files=args.patch_files,\\n                copy_root_dir=args.copy_root_dir,\\n            )\\n===== domains/harness.py =====\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\n\\n# Add the project root to Python path\\nsys.path.append(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), ".."))\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport importlib\\nimport importlib.util\\nimport os\\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\\nfrom datetime import datetime\\nimport pandas as pd\\nfrom hydra import compose, initialize_config_dir\\nfrom types import ModuleType\\n\\n\\ndef get_dataset(domain, subset=""):\\n    df = None\\n    if "imo_" in domain:\\n        df = pd.read_csv(f"./domains/imo/{domain.split(\\\'_\\\')[-1]}bench{subset}.csv", dtype=str)\\n    elif domain in ["search_arena", "paper_review"]:\\n        df = pd.read_csv(f"./domains/{domain}/dataset{subset}.csv", dtype=str)\\n    return df\\n\\ndef run_agent(TaskAgent, model, row, evals_folder, format_input_dict, question_id_col):\\n    question_id = row[question_id_col]\\n    chat_history_path = os.path.join(evals_folder, f"chat_history_{question_id}.md")\\n    agent = TaskAgent(model=model, chat_history_file=chat_history_path)\\n    inputs = format_input_dict(row)\\n    prediction, _ = agent.forward(inputs)\\n    return prediction\\n\\n\\ndef load_task_agent(agent_path: str):\\n    """\\n    agent_path can be:\\n      - a python file path: ./task_agent.py or /abs/path/task_agent.py\\n      - a module path: proofgrader.task_agent or my_pkg.my_agent\\n    Returns: TaskAgent class\\n    """\\n    # Case 1: looks like a file path or exists on disk\\n    if agent_path.endswith(".py") or os.path.exists(agent_path):\\n        abs_path = os.path.abspath(agent_path)\\n        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("agent_module", abs_path)\\n        if spec is None or spec.loader is None:\\n            raise ImportError(f"Could not load spec from file: {abs_path}")\\n        mod = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)\\n        spec.loader.exec_module(mod)\\n        if not hasattr(mod, "TaskAgent"):\\n            raise AttributeError(f"No TaskAgent found in file: {abs_path}")\\n        return mod.TaskAgent\\n\\n    # Case 2: interpret as module path\\n    mod = importlib.import_module(agent_path)\\n    if not hasattr(mod, "TaskAgent"):\\n        raise AttributeError(f"No TaskAgent found in module: {agent_path}")\\n    return mod.TaskAgent\\n\\ndef harness(\\n    agent_path="./task_agent.py",\\n    output_dir="./outputs",\\n    run_id=None,\\n    domain="search_arena",\\n    num_samples=-1,\\n    save_interval=100,\\n    num_workers=5,\\n    resume_from=None,\\n    subset="",\\n    proofs_dname=None,\\n):\\n    # Dynamically import functions based on the domain\\n    utils_prefix = domain.split("_", 1)[1] + "_" if domain.startswith("imo_") else ""\\n    domain_folder = domain.split(\\\'_\\\')[0] if "imo_" in domain else domain\\n    utils_module_path = f"domains.{domain_folder}.{utils_prefix}utils"\\n    utils_module = importlib.import_module(utils_module_path)\\n    format_input_dict = utils_module.format_input_dict\\n    question_id_col = utils_module.QUESTION_ID\\n    model = utils_module.MODEL\\n\\n    # Load TaskAgent either from a file path or an importable module path\\n    TaskAgent = load_task_agent(agent_path)\\n\\n    # Specify output folder\\n    if resume_from:\\n        output_folder = os.path.abspath(resume_from)\\n    else:\\n        run_id = (\\n            datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f") if run_id is None else run_id\\n        )\\n        output_folder = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), output_dir, run_id)\\n\\n    # Create output folder\\n    evals_folder = os.path.join(output_folder, "agent_evals")\\n    os.makedirs(evals_folder, exist_ok=True)\\n    output_path = os.path.join(output_folder, "predictions.csv")\\n\\n    # Load existing predictions if available\\n    if os.path.exists(output_path):\\n        existing_df = pd.read_csv(output_path, dtype=str)\\n        completed_ids = set(\\n            existing_df[~existing_df["prediction"].isna()][question_id_col]\\n        )\\n    else:\\n        existing_df = None\\n        completed_ids = set()\\n\\n    # Get dataset\\n    if proofs_dname:\\n        dataset = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(proofs_dname, "predictions.csv"), dtype=str)\\n        dataset["Response"] = dataset["prediction"].copy()\\n        dataset.drop(columns=["prediction"], inplace=True)\\n    else:\\n        dataset = get_dataset(domain=domain, subset=subset)\\n    if num_samples > 0:\\n        dataset = dataset[:num_samples]\\n\\n    # Add a prediction column\\n    if existing_df is not None:\\n        dataset = dataset.merge(\\n            existing_df[[question_id_col, "prediction"]], on=question_id_col, how="left"\\n        )\\n    else:\\n        dataset["prediction"] = None\\n\\n    predictions = dataset["prediction"].tolist()\\n    futures = []\\n\\n    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=num_workers) as executor:\\n        for i, row in dataset.iterrows():\\n            if (\\n                pd.notna(row["prediction"]) or row[question_id_col] in completed_ids\\n            ):  # pyright: ignore\\n                continue\\n            futures.append(\\n                (\\n                    i,\\n                    executor.submit(\\n                        run_agent,\\n                        TaskAgent, model, row, evals_folder,\\n                        format_input_dict, question_id_col,\\n                    ),\\n                )\\n            )\\n\\n        for idx, future in futures:\\n            prediction = future.result()\\n            predictions[idx] = prediction\\n\\n            if (idx + 1) % save_interval == 0:\\n                dataset["prediction"] = predictions\\n                dataset.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\\n                print(f"Checkpoint saved to {output_path}")\\n\\n    # Final save\\n    dataset["prediction"] = predictions\\n    dataset.to_csv(output_path, index=False)\\n    print(f"Final predictions saved to {output_path}")\\n\\n    return output_folder\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == "__main__":\\n    # Parse command-line arguments\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\\n        description="Evaluate a system on the search arena dataset."\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--agent_path", type=str, default="./task_agent.py", help="Path to the agent"\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--output_dir", type=str, default="./outputs", help="Output directory"\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument("--run_id", type=str, default=None, help="Run ID")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--domain",\\n        type=str,\\n        choices=[\\n            "search_arena",\\n            "paper_review",\\n            "balrog_babyai",\\n            "balrog_babaisai",\\n            "balrog_minihack",\\n            "balrog_nle",\\n            "genesis_go2walking",\\n            "genesis_go2walkback",\\n            "genesis_go2hop",\\n            "imo_grading",\\n            "imo_proof",\\n            "imo_proof_grading",  # To grade generated proofs with an agent\\n        ],\\n        required=True,\\n        help="Domain to evaluate",\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--num_samples",\\n        type=int,\\n        default=-1,\\n        help="Number of samples to evaluate, -1 for all",\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--save_interval", type=int, default=100, help="Save to CSV every n samples"\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--num_workers", type=int, default=5, help="Number of parallel workers"\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--resume_from",\\n        type=str,\\n        default=None,\\n        help="Path to an existing output folder to resume from",\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--subset", type=str, default="", help="Subset of the dataset to evaluate"\\n    )\\n    parser.add_argument(\\n        "--proofs_dname", type=str, default="", help="Path to the directory containing proofs to grade (for imo_proof_grading)"\\n    )\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    domain = args.domain\\n   # Make proofs_dname required for imo_proof_grading\\n    if domain == "imo_proof_grading" and not args.proofs_dname:\\n        parser.error("--proofs_dname is required when domain is \\\'imo_proof_grading\\\'")\\n\\n    # Human preferences domains\\n    if domain in ["search_arena", "paper_review", "imo_grading", "imo_proof", "imo_proof_grading"]:\\n        output_folder = harness(\\n            agent_path=args.agent_path,\\n            output_dir=args.output_dir,\\n            run_id=args.run_id,\\n            domain=args.domain,\\n            num_samples=args.num_samples,\\n            save_interval=args.save_interval,\\n            num_workers=args.num_workers,\\n            resume_from=args.resume_from,\\n            subset=args.subset,\\n            proofs_dname=args.proofs_dname,\\n        )\\n\\n    # Balrog game domains\\n    elif "balrog" in domain:\\n        from domains.balrog.eval import harness_balrog\\n\\n        env_name = domain.split("_")[-1]\\n        config_dir = os.path.join(os.getcwd(), "./domains/balrog/config")\\n        with initialize_config_dir(config_dir=config_dir, version_base="1.1"):\\n            cfg = compose(\\n                config_name="config",\\n                overrides=[\\n                    f"eval.output_dir={args.output_dir}",\\n                    f"eval.num_workers={args.num_workers}",\\n                    f"envs.names={env_name}",\\n                    f"eval.run_id={args.run_id if args.run_id is not None else \\\'null\\\'}",\\n                ]\\n                + (\\n                    [f"eval.num_episodes.{env_name}={args.num_samples}"]\\n                    if args.num_samples > 0\\n                    else []\\n                )\\n===== domains/report.py =====\\nimport os\\nimport shutil\\nimport sys\\n\\n# Add the project root to Python path\\nsys.path.append(os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "../.."))\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport importlib.util\\nimport json\\nimport pandas as pd\\n\\n\\ndef report(\\n    dname,\\n    domain,\\n    suffix="",  # suffix to the predictions{suffix}.csv filename\\n):\\n    # Dynamically import functions based on the domain\\n    utils_prefix = domain.split("_", 1)[1] + "_" if domain.startswith("imo_") else ""\\n    domain_folder = domain.split(\\\'_\\\')[0] if "imo_" in domain else domain\\n    utils_module_path = f"domains.{domain_folder}.{utils_prefix}utils"\\n    utils_module = importlib.import_module(utils_module_path)\\n    ground_truth_key = utils_module.GROUND_TRUTH_KEY\\n    question_id_col = utils_module.QUESTION_ID\\n\\n    # Load and process the data\\n    path = os.path.join(dname, f"predictions{suffix}.csv")\\n    df = pd.read_csv(path, dtype=str)\\n    df = df[df["prediction"] != ""].copy()  # Filter out rows with NA predictions\\n    df["prediction"] = df["prediction"].str.strip().str.lower()\\n    df[ground_truth_key] = df[ground_truth_key].str.strip().str.lower()\\n    df["match"] = df[ground_truth_key] == df["prediction"]\\n\\n    # Calculate overall accuracy\\n    accuracy = df["match"].mean()\\n    total_correct = int(df["match"].sum())\\n    total = len(df)\\n    print(f"Accuracy: {accuracy:.3f}, Total correct: {total_correct} / {total}")\\n\\n    # Calculate Mean Absolute Error (MAE)\\n    if domain == "imo_grading":\\n        try:\\n            max_error = 7\\n            df["prediction_points"] = (\\n                df["prediction"]\\n                .map({"incorrect": 0, "partial": 1, "almost": 6, "correct": 7})\\n                .astype(float)\\n            )\\n            df[\\\'reward_points\\\'] = (\\n                df[ground_truth_key]\\n                .map({"incorrect": 0, "partial": 1, "almost": 6, "correct": 7})\\n                .astype(float)\\n            )\\n            df["error"] = abs(df["prediction_points"] - df[\\\'reward_points\\\'])\\n            df["error"] = df["error"].fillna(max_error)\\n            mae = df["error"].mean() / max_error\\n            print(f"Normalized Mean Absolute Error (MAE): {mae:.3f}")\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            mae = None\\n            print("Error: Could not calculate MAE for IMO grading")\\n            print(e)\\n\\n    # Accuracy by label\\n    label_accuracies = df.groupby(ground_truth_key)["match"].mean()\\n    label_counts = df[ground_truth_key].value_counts()\\n\\n    print("\\\\nAccuracy by label:")\\n    label_report = {}\\n    labels = set(df[ground_truth_key].unique())\\n    for label in labels:\\n        # True Positive (TP): predicted = label and actual = label\\n        tp = ((df["prediction"] == label) & (df[ground_truth_key] == label)).sum()\\n        # False Positive (FP): predicted = label but actual ≠ label\\n        fp = ((df["prediction"] == label) & (df[ground_truth_key] != label)).sum()\\n        # False Negative (FN): predicted ≠ label but actual = label\\n        fn = ((df["prediction"] != label) & (df[ground_truth_key] == label)).sum()\\n        # Precision and Recall\\n        precision = tp / (tp + fp) if (tp + fp) > 0 else 0.0\\n        recall = tp / (tp + fn) if (tp + fn) > 0 else 0.0\\n        correct_label = tp\\n        total_label = int(label_counts.get(label, 0))\\n\\n        print(\\n            f"  Label: {label} - Precision: {precision:.3f}, Recall: {recall:.3f}, Correct: {correct_label} / {total_label}"\\n        )\\n        label_report[str(label)] = {\\n            "precision": float(precision),\\n            "recall": float(recall),\\n            "correct": int(correct_label),\\n            "total": int(total_label),\\n        }\\n\\n    # Compute label distributions for winner and prediction\\n    winner_distribution = df[ground_truth_key].value_counts(normalize=True).to_dict()\\n    prediction_distribution = df["prediction"].value_counts(normalize=True).to_dict()\\n\\n    print("\\\\nDistribution of ground truth labels:")\\n    for label, freq in winner_distribution.items():\\n        print(f"  {label}: {freq:.3f}")\\n\\n    print("\\\\nDistribution of prediction labels:")\\n    for label, freq in prediction_distribution.items():\\n        print(f"  {label}: {freq:.3f}")\\n\\n    # Compute expected random guess accuracy using winner label distribution\\n    random_guess_accuracy = sum(p**2 for p in winner_distribution.values())\\n    print(\\n        f"\\\\nExpected random guess accuracy (based on ground_truth_key label distribution): {random_guess_accuracy:.3f}"\\n    )\\n\\n    # Build the report dictionary\\n    report = {\\n        "overall_accuracy": float(accuracy),\\n        **({"normalized_mean_absolute_error": mae} if domain == "imo_grading" else {}),\\n        "total_correct": total_correct,\\n        "total": total,\\n        "accuracy_by_ground_truth": label_report,\\n        "label_distribution": {\\n            "ground_truth": winner_distribution,\\n            "prediction": prediction_distribution,\\n        },\\n        "random_guess_accuracy": random_guess_accuracy,\\n        "question_ids_failed": [\\n            row[question_id_col] for _, row in df[df["match"] == False].iterrows()\\n        ],\\n        "question_ids_passed": [\\n            row[question_id_col] for _, row in df[df["match"] == True].iterrows()\\n        ],\\n    }\\n\\n    # Save the report as a JSON file\\n    report_path = os.path.join(dname, f"report{suffix}.json")\\n    with open(report_path, "w") as f:\\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=4)\\n\\n    return report, report_path\\n\\ndef report_imo_proof(dname):\\n    # Grade the generated proofs\\n    from domains.harness import harness\\n    output_folder = harness(\\n        domain="imo_proof_grading",\\n        agent_path="proofgrader.task_agent",\\n        proofs_dname=dname,\\n        output_dir=dname,\\n        run_id="gradings",\\n    )\\n    # Get report of grading\\n    from domains.imo.proof_eval import report_proof_grading\\n
